Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The radioactivity operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 98. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 433 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the radioactivity space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 98 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 433. QED.
